~Dahaka nake cewa masu karatu kubiyoni sannu Ahankali danjin ~yayaxata~ ~kasance~ A *TSAKA MAI WUYA~*
بسم الله الر حمن الر حيم،

01
Yarone matashi d'an kimanin shekaru Ashirin da ukku Aduniya" yana sanye cikin qananan kaya farare tasssss! riqe da littafai Ahannayensa, tafiya yake cikin yanayin nutsuwa da ginanniyar tarbiya" hankalinsa kwance xahiri bashida wata damuwa" wani gida me qaramin qarfi da rashin yalwar Arxiki yashiga da cewa "salamu Alaikum" amma shiru bawanda ya amsa hakan yasanyashi fad'awa gidan batare da naxarin komaiba" wata dattijuwa ce xaune tsakiyar gidan me kimanin shekaru hamsin da Aihuwa" tana rungome da bahausar shinkafa me jahhh da tsakuwa ta sadda kai qasa batare da tsintar shinkafar da tarunguma ba" cakkkk yajah yatsaya yana naxarinta' daga bisani yarankwafo cikin yanayin damuwa yace innah!

Firgigit tad'ago tare da yin yaqe tace Muhammad kadawo? Shima yaqen yayi yace Eh nadawo tund'axu! murmushi tayi tace sannu da hanya yakaratun? Uhmu segodiyar Allah innah" shiru yad'an ratsasu nadaqiqu yakatse dacewa Inna haryanxu baki d'ora Abunciba yamma nayi ko bakida lafiya ne!?

Idonta kamar xaikawo qwallah tace lafiyana qalau muhammad' babu gawayin da xand'ora inajiran qanwarkane tun d'axu nace taje tasamo ledoji naga ko xaidafa mana shinkafarnan tunsafe rabon mu da Abunci" da Alamar damuwa yace haba innah nace kidaina aikenta A unguwar nan tsintar ledoji kidinga bari nasamo ita macce ne bekamata anasakata kowane aiki ba" da yanayin damuwar itama tace Muhammad dolece tasanyani nikaina ba Asan raina bane" littafan sa ya'ajiye be furta komaiba yafita" yanafita yaga qanwarsa tadawo cike da kulawa tace yah muh'd sannu daxuwa! Murmushin sa yafad'ad'a kana yace sannu qanwata yana qoqarin karb'ar buhun da tayotsince tsince dashi!

Bayan yakarb'a sukashiga yana tambayarta me akayi masu A class? Seda ta yatsina fuska tace komai! Xare ido yayi yace  komai shin? Tace eh wani malami ne Kawai yashiga yamuna tatsuniya" qwafa yayi da ido aransa yace yaushe nigeria zataci gaba!? ko yaushene talaka zaihuta Aqasar nan? duba da ilmin ma ankasa bashi! Inkaga yaro da ilmi me kyau bincika waye ubanshi? Haqiqa bazakasamu talaka bane'' jin shirun Muhammad yayiyawa tace yah meyasa kayi shiru!? Yaqe yayi yace Rayyanah Karatun talaka kebani tausayi yau sati ukku kina zuwa makaranta babu karatun kirki ahaka har qarshen zango yaqare baku gane komai ba" narasa gano bakin zaren shin kodan bayaransu Amakarantun gwamnatine sukayi shiru ilmin maras galihu nazama tarihi!? Kokuma sun manta A kyauta sukasamu ilmi lokacin su sir Ahamd bello" su tafawa b'alewa" su yakubu gawan" se yaji hawaye sun wanke mai fuska.....

Hannuta takai A hankali tashare hawayen tace yah muh'd kayi shiru kadaina zubda hawayenka kullum sekayi kuka innace ba'akaratu makaranta kuma kana koyamun inayi.…rungumota jikinsa yayi yace Rayyana ke in ina koyamaki wasufa?! Dayawa kuke amakaranta Rayyanah! kuma Annabi muh'd yana cewa d'ayanku bazaiyi imaniba harsai yasoma d'an u'wansa abunda yakeso wakanshii.." Shiru yaratsasu duka biyun can mamansu taleqo taga halin da suke ciki A sanyaye tace kuyi haquri muh'd ita duniya haka take wannan Abun da kake gani zezama tarihi duk Wanda kukeji Ana cema wani sedayasan duniya" kuma zaku samu ilmi dukkk dacewa kunxo babin qaddara mai wuyar sha'ani" haka dai sukayi zaune suna jajinta lamarin can innah tace muh'd maganar hajiya rabi kojiya tazoooo.

Iskar bakinsa yahuro yace innah kiyi haquri banason kuyi aikinan zuciyata bazata juri kuna maula gidan waniba, se naga kamar Wasiyar Abbana nake tozartawa bayan qasa tashafe idonsa" kamar tayi kuka tace Muhammad babu wanda yake kauce qaddararsa' kuma aikin zaifi mana kocikin mu muke cikawa yafi muna Azumin da babu lada....

Gwaron numfashi yasauke yace shikenan innah Allah yasanya kufara cikin nasara yayimun Arxikin samun aikin da zanriqe ku""""Amin tafad'a tare da sanya mashi Albarka.…..


Bayan kwana biyu innah da Rayyanah suka shirya cikin wata shatattar atamfa da qiris takejira ta yage, suka kama hanya sosai suke tafiya ba niyar shiga keke npape har suka kai wata unguwa mabera" unguwar shiru' kamar bakowa kana kowane gida ka duba kasan na mehannu da miyane da haka suka shiga wani katafaren gida na wani sanannen d'an siyasa A Nigeria" yana saman bene yana kallon masu shige da fice nagidan yahango innah da rayyana" zumbur Alhajin yamiqe yana duban Rayyana da son sanin wacece..? Nan take yakira amuntaccen yaronsa jabeer yace idan sunfito yabimashi sawunsu asan A inasuke...? Jabeer kamar yatafi yadai tsaya yace Yallab'ai tsohuwar ko yarinyar" tsaki yajahhhhh me sauti yace mezanyi da tsohuwar yarinyar dai" galala yasaki baki yace yallab'ai batafi 11 ba fa mezakagane'""" dariya yasaki yadaki kafad'arsa yace jabeer.......jabeer

الحمد لله وبه نستعين،وصلي الله علي سيد نا محمد و علي اله و صحبه وسلم تسليما.
بسم الله الر حمن الر حيم،

02
Yace Jabeer...jabeer ai dodo yafison nariqa Zina da yaran da basahaura15yes ba, shikuma yayitabani sa'a akan harkar siyasana kana sunana na fad'ad'a a bakunan mutanen duniya...! Dan haka aikin kad'aya ka bunciko wacece...?

Sosai yaqyal qyala dariya yace kamar kaikaxon dakanka Alhaji! Koyau kakeson kwana da itah xakasamu! Cikin jindad'i yace nasani jabeer d'ina' yana dariya Yafice" sukuma sunashiga suka gaisar da hajiya rabi,,, cikin girmamawa da Rashin qyamar talaka takarb'esu kana suka rab'e gefee..! Bayan gajeren shiru da yaratsa su hajiya rabi tace innar rayyana muh'd ya amunce da maganarmu kenan"!? Cikin xumud'i tace Eh hajiya! thommm Masha Allah wanke wanke da share sharene naki sune babu meyi..

Sunkuyar dakai tayi qasa tace ngd hajiya Allah yabar xumunci yaqara arxiki da yalwar lafiya...! Amin tace taredamiqewa ta nuna mata gurin aikinta, bayan taga komai sukadawo suka xauna daga bisani suka wuce gida da Xummar gobe xatayo samakko! Dan fara aikinta.

Koda suka isah gidan muh'd na kuka me sautiii cikin raxani sukayo kanshi" inna dai batace komaiba se rayyanah ce taxame daga jikin innar tace cikin murya me rauni da rawar kuka yah muh'd meyasameka? Wayab'ata maka rai Dan Allah...jikinsa yarungumota yace rayyanah bawanda yab'ata mun rai haka kawai nake tirrrr dakasawata gareku!!!

Hannunsa tariqo cikin sigar hankali tace yaya karkace haka mukam baka kasa mana da komaiba Alkhairanka da tarin soyayyarka muka sani bakasawa ba, kukanka shine tashin hankalina dana innarmu, yah muh'd mu maida Al'amarin mu ga Allah watarana halin da muke ciki xaixama labari....takuma kai hannu tashare masa hawaye sukaxauna xaman tunanin Abubuwa dayawa dasuka faru bayan qasa tashafe jigon su Aduniya...!

 wayewar gari tun safe muh'd da rayyanah suka tashi suka share gida tass kana yatayata wanke wanke sunayine yadubeta yace rayyana inaburin nayi kud'i sosai sabida kawai kijj dad'i A duniya.. Murmushi tayi tace nikam banaso kayi kud'i sabida Annabi yanacewa daga cikin fitinar Al'ummata Akwai dukiya banaso tashagaltar da qaunarmu" tagumi yayi yana kallonta tabushe mar idoó yaqyal qyala dariya tare dacewa mekikso nayi tommm inbakud'i ba? Sadda kai qasa tayi tana wasa dayatsanata kana tace mutuwane banaso kayi yah muh'd domin kai nakeyiwa kallon Abbana!... Wani Abu yaji yatokare ransa amma yadake yace baxan mutu nabarki ba rayyana namaki Alqawari se naxama bango agareki xanxama tamkar bawa rayyanah idan harxakiyi dariya.

Yatsanta tamiqo tace muqullah Alqawari bamusu yamiqa mata nashi bayan sun qullah tace yah muh'd dukkkk yadda duniya tarikice da qulle qullen makircinta katuna ranar yau domin kuwa Alqawari Abun tambayane gaban Allah S.W!

Idonnnnsa yaji sunkawo qwallah yace Allah kaxama shaida idan ina numfashi ina da sukuni rayyanah baxatayi kukaba...xatayi magana innarsu takatseta da cewa Rayyanah kishirya lokacin tafiya yayi" cikin girmamawa tace thommm innah bawani b'ata lokaci tamiqe shima yamiqe' suka shirya atare suka fitoooo muh'd harxaifita se yace xega inda xasufara aiki sannan yawuce makaranta! Inna ba musu tace shikenan muje" haka kuwa akayi...tafe suke suna fira cikin qaunayya harsuka isah...…

Bakin gidan yatsaya suka shiga ciki sunashiga Alahji na fitowa sashensa harqasa inna da rayyana suka gaidashi  ya amsa but hankalinshi nakan sabuwar harqallar shi datahanashi bacci ganin farko da idonsa suka mata'" jin shirun da yad'auke yasa inna Jan rayyana sukatafi.... tana bada baya yawani had'a yawuuuu da kyar kana A fili yace wajibina ne najawoku jiki domin jiran yahuce shike kawo rabon wani....suko bayan sunshiga aiki suka fara kamar yadda aka nunawa innah kana ba kissa tsakaninta da Allah takeyi! Bayan ta kammalah hajiya rabi tafito tayaba sosai da aikin daga bisani tace taxo tagabatar da ita ga megidan!.

Cike da sakin fuska yaamshe su kana yamata fatan nasara da haquri' har ga Allah tajj dad'i tayi godiya sosai harxasu miqe yace ke Dota yasunanki....

Cikin sigar yarinta tace Rayyanah wani yarrrrrr yaji kana yakoma namiji yace Abbanki yasamaki suna maidad'i kuma mai darajah..murmushi tayi suka fita yana kallon tafiya me cike da nutsuwa.........

03
Yana kallon tafiya me cike da nutsuwa..!har seda ganin sa yadaina hangota sannan yad'auke kansa!!!!

Gefen muh'd ya isa makaranta lafiya, kana cikin sa'a ba'afara lecture ba, xaune yayi can baya yana "bud'a hand out" d'insa batare da shiga sabgar kowa ba, yana haka wani matashin saurayi da bayawuce shekarunsa" yadaki kafad'arsa dacewa shaihi.." Shaihi" murmushin sa yafad'ad'a tare da cewa bro Ahmad! Ya yau"? dubansa yayi sosai yace lafiya qalau but inaga bakasan wainar da aketoyawa ba shaihi...!?

Cikin miqa nutsuwarsa yace mekefaruwa Ahamad..? Zab'en sossa president xa'ayi..tsaki yajah iya leb'e kana yace su yashafa ni bana cikin kowa Allah basu sa'a...barama nawucewata gida dan yau school d'in ba karatu" dariya Ahamad yayi yace shaihi dagaske wucewa xakayi..? D'an bud'e baki yayi yace ah mexanjira bayan naje neman Abunda xankorewa kaina quda" silent smile yasaki yace gaskiya ne amma tun d'axuuuu naga mutuniyar ka nasafa da marwa yakamata ko gaisawa kuyi!!!!

Gwaron numfashi yasaki yace Ahamad rufamun Asiri ni dukkk Abunda nayi lissafi nakeyi kamar yadda nafad'a abaya gaisuwar musulunci xatariqa sadamu maganar so bayanxu ba inada u'wa da qanwa wanda suke buqatar soyayyata" sannan ko bawannan Salmah tafiqarfina takowane siga...! Dubansa Ahamad yayi yace soyayyar u'wa da qanwa daban, soyayyar saurayi da budurwa daban shaihi" magnar fin qarfi kuma Ajiyeta gefe domin So beduba wannan..! Kana Sanin kan kane dukkk kud'in mahaifin salmah besata shiga jerun watsatsun y'anmata ba! Haka dubun jama'a masu kud'i dagata kebinta suna tsumayin samun tamusu magana ko saud'aya amma batabasu dama ba....! Shaihi kayi naxari akan tayin salmah"""Thom Ahamad xanyi sedai inaso kaima kayi naxarin kasancewar kowace qwarya y'ar uwarta take bi"

Ido Ahamad yaxura masa shikuma yadafashi dacewa nabarka lafiya...! Bayan tafeeyarsa kad'an Salmah taxooo tare da rakiyar qawarta Zareena" cikin sakin fuska tace Ahamad yakukayi da shaihina..? Fuska bayabo yace haryanxu bawani cigaba maganarsa tabaya itace sedai kiyi ta Addu'a...murmushi tayi tace ina kanyi kuma watarana xataxo nayi nasara shaihi yakarb'i soyayyata....

Aransa tausayi tabashi A fili yace hakane salmah domin kin mallaki komai da namiji ke buqata..! Uhmmm wani dai Ahamad bcx nasani ban mallaki komaiba tunda yaushekara d'aya nakasa samun Aron rana d'aya da xanyi magana da shaihina" murmushi yayi batare da furta komai ba" xareenah tamkar xuciyarta tafashe Dan baqin ciki! Wai kamar salmah ace tana maular so gurin yaron da bewuce tataka tawuce ba"" salmah naganin xarina but ko Ajikinta....!

Harseda taqare tace muje xareenah! Batace komaiba  tayi gaba" Haka sukayi tafiya med'an tsayi ba mecewa komai seda harsuka shiga mota xareena ta kwanta ta yatsina fuska tace gaskiya salmah A jinki bekammala ba! Seda tad'au mintuna biyu xuwa ukku sannan tace me kikace xareenah..!? Cike da jin haushi tace shaihi nace!!!!

Sanyayyar ajiyar xuciya tasaki tace wow!! xareena kamar kinsan shinake fasalawa da kalar rayuwar da xamuyi gaba...! Tsaki tajah me sauti tace salmah Dan Allah kimanta yaronnan ga maxaje nan dayawa nasonki dai daike masu kud'i domin yau kyau namaxa bayawuce naira!!! Numfashi tasauke tace A gurinki xareenah...but duk duniya bawanda nake gani namiji kamar muh'd maganar kud'i duniya Akesamu kuma nan Akebari, xareena bantab'a ji nafi muh'd da komai ba Dan haka inkinaso mucigaba da xama Alkhairan muh'd sukasance Abokan firarki idan kuwa bahaka ba xanbarki har Abada...!!!!!

Shiru xareeena tayi Dan dagaske salmah ta Afka Tarkon son muh'd kana tace shikenan salmah kicigaba da son shaihi..! A yanayin fushi tace basaikince ba Xareena domin mutuwa kad'ai ke iya dakatar dani....! Ta tada mota suka tafi ba maicewa kowa komai harsuka isah gida, Anan ma parking tayi tafito da qarfi! Abbanta naxaune yana karatun jarida yaga shigewarta fuuuuuuuu,,,,!

Murya yad'aga yace jabeerrrrrrrr! Dasauri yace na'am Alhji lafiya"? hankali tashe yace bi sawun salmah kace ina kiranta...! Dasauri yashiga yace ma hajiya rabi ina salmah..!?

Cikin Rashin fahimta tace tana makaranta jabeer lafiya...?

04
Cikin Rashin fahimta tace tana makaranta jabeer lafiya..?! Lafiya qalau hajiya Alhajine yace yaga shigowarta raxane! Cikin rashin sukuni tace Raxane..? Eh hajiya! Cikin yanayin mamaki tace ikon Allah Inanan banga shigowarta ba But  bara naduba! Thom duba mugani Bawani b'ata lokaci tataka benen da xaisadata da y'arta amma Abun mamaki d'akin wayam! Tana fitowa da xummar bashi Amsa Zareenah tashigo" da wayoyin salam da hand bag d'inta" daga hajiya har jabeer jira suke tafurta wani Abu akan salmah" amma setajah tatsaya shiru yaratsasu.......! Jabeer ne yakatse dacewa xareenah ina salamar!? Dubansa tayi tare dacewa Abunda xantambaya kenan yanxu tashigo ina bayanta...! Numfashi hajiya tasauke kana tace meyafaru kukashigo daban daban!?

D'an saitawa xareena tayi tace cikinta ke murd'awa shiyasa' murmushi hajiya tayi kana jabeer yace Thom "Alhmdullah yafice" xareena kuma tayi gurin hutawar salmah Dan tasam batawuce can..cikin sa'a kuwa tana xaune dirsha tasunkuyar dakai qasa" xareenah ta marairaice murya tace salmah kiyi haquri bansan maganan xaiyi kauri haka ba! D'agowa tayi tace yawuce xareenah amma dan Allah karkiqara kushe "shaihi" gabana ina sonshi fiye da yadda kike tsammani kitayani Addu'a Allah yabani muh'd!

Rungumeta tayi tace insha Allah salmah namaki Alqawarin Addu'a daganan suka fara fira..gefen muh'd yana isa gida yacire kayanshi yad'auko kayan Rayyana da inna yana wankewa da y'ar omon da yaiqo...! bayan yakammala yad'auki baro da jalka biyu naruwa yadoshi rijjya dan d'aukar Ruwan dayake siyarwa A unguwanni, har magrib tagabato yadawo da cinkin dayayi d'ari da hamsin. naira hamsin yasaka Ah banki, naira d'ari kuma yasiyo gari da quli yaxauna ba'afi mintuna ukku ba Sega Rayyanah da inna washe baki yayi yace sannu innah? Ya aiki..? Lafiya qalau muh'd ta amsa dashi kanaa yamaida kallonsa ga rayyana yaga se haki take Alamar gajiy..matsowa yayi daf da itah yace rayyana ba gaisuwa!? Murmushi tayi seda ilahirin haqoranta suka bayyana tarufe fuskarta tace najira kaqare da innarmu ne!

shima Murmushi yayi yace bakomai kind'ebo gajiya rayyana! Suna haka aka Kira sallah duka miqewa sukayi xuwa ga ubangijinsu bayan sunkammala bacci yad'auke Rayyana! Haka dai lokaci yafara tafiya har 12 biyun dare ganin nepa basukawo wuta ba muh' d yatada rayyana yace tayi wanka xafi yayiwa' cikin magagin bacci tamiqe yahad'a mata ruwa tayi wanka yaqara gyara mata shimfid'a ta kwanta yanatofa Mata falaqi da nassss bayan bacci yad'auketa yasanya hannayensa yana dannah tafunan qafafuwanta" Ahankali ta  motsa cikin muryar bacci tace yah muh'd kaje ka kwanta kajiiiii! Ngd da kulawarka Allah yasanya mumutu tare!!!

Murmushi yayi yace Amin Amin Rayyanah kuyi haquri dani nikaina takaicin kaina nake da nabarku kuna qangin bauta gidan Wani" xaune tamiqe tana dubansa tace yah muh'd kadaina jin damuwa Dan Allah" kulawarka kad'ai kwanciyar hankaline A garemu!! Shiru yayi yana shafa kanta tausayinta na fisgarshi yamaida kallonsa ga innarsu aransa yace Allah sarki innarmu ubangiji yabani damar kare y'ancinku....! Haka har gwanin iyasata yasacesu suna tsananin tausayin junansu!

Bayan wayewar gari suka shiga shirye shiryen tafiya yauma muh'd seda yamasu rakiya har qofar gidan kana yajuya xuwa makaranfa yana tafe yana tausayin qanwarsa tun A wannan shekarun tanasahan wuyar duniya! Har yaisa Yanashiga sega Salmah da fara'a tace barka shaihi!? D'auke kai yayi yace barka yakike....? Cikin sanyin jiki tace Dan Allah muh'd katsaya kasaurareni..! Cak yatsaya tare dacewa inajinki kiyi sauri....araunane tace shaihi Xanyi tafiya" juyowa yayi yadubeta duban mekikeso idan xaki tafi!? Ta sadda kai qasa tace numberka nakeso!? Banida waya salmah kiyi haquri...d'agowa tayi idooo cike da qwallah tace muh'd turkia xanje nayi degree d'ina Dan Allah kabani kogaisawa mayi...!

Numfashi yasauke yace banida waya salmah ko babu xanbaki..? Kaitagirgixa Alamar A'a! Yace thommm xan iya tafiya!? Muryarta nasarqewa tace Eh shaihi ngd da lokacin da kabani..! Tafiya yayi Tana kallonsa Afili tace muh'd xancigaba da Dakon soyayyarka tsawon lokacin da Allah yaso mukasance Aminan juna..tana juyawa kuma xareena tace muje salmah watarana muh'd xaisoki! Cikin mutuwar jiki tace uhmmm xareena Allah ka amunce min namallaki shaihi kodakuwa shine Ajalina" qwafa tayi batace komaiba se mamakin salmah dake lilo Aqashin xuciyarta......! 

Hakadai Rayuwa tasoma tafiya Bayan wattanni shidda inna da rayyana sosai sukejin dad'in Aiki gidan hajiya Rabi" hakama muh'd yabarkomai gurin Allah ita kuwa salmah tana can turkiya xuciyarta na begen shaihi" yau laraba da sanyin safiya innar rayyana takammala shara da wanke wankenta Alhajj yace mata waye wanda yake rakota da safe...?! Cikin jindad'i tace muh'd ne yayan Rayyana! Dafara'a yace haba inaganin hankalinsa wane Aiki yakeyine....!? Duk da begirmeta ba cikin sigar ladabi tace karatu yakeyi Alhaji! Murmushi yayi yace in babu damuwa kice yaxooo yad'an mini wasu Ayukkah kafun yarona jabeer yadawo!!!! Cike dajin dad'i tace babu damuwa Alhaji insha Allah gobe xanxo dashi ngd Allah yaqara sutura...amin yace yanawucewa! Bayan magrib Inna da rayyana cike da farinki suka koma gidah! Muh'd nashigowa Inna talabarta masa Amma seyasha murrrrrrrr!

Itama had'e fuska tayi tace muh'd baka farinciki ne..? Shiru yayi sannan yace A'a inna nibanason walaqanci kuma kinsan masukud'in yanxu gani suke kamar su sukabama kansu babu medarajah A idanuwansu se me kud'i d'anuwansu" Dan haka inaroqonki kisanar mai cewa nagode datayinsa karatun dake gabana ma aikine!!!!

Kallo inna taqare masa kana tace muh'd gobe kashirya fita Aiki gidan Alhaji idan kuma bahakaa ba xansab'a maka! Cikin ladabi yace tuba nake Allah yahuci zuciyar innata kuma xanfita aikin insha Allah yayishiru"

Tausayinsa innataji kana tace muh'd ngd da biyayyarka gareni da dad'i daba dad'i duk kanemun Ina maku fatan nasara dakai da y'aruwarka Allah yaqara shiryaku shirin Addini..! Tare suka amsa da Amin dagabisani inna tafara yaba halayen hajiya rabi da mijinta Alhaji. Bayan wayewar gari suka d'unguma sukaje gidan hajia rabi, muh'd yasha mamaki ganin mekud'i shahararren d'an siyasa da talauci be rab'a ba kana duniya kedamawa dashi baya qyamar talaka" yadda yatarbesu cikin farinciki kad'ai xainuna ma baid'auki duniya da girma ba! Kana cikin mintuna yagabatar ma muh'd kansa dakuma Abunda yake buqata yadinga mai nawani lokaci muh'd fuska fad'ad'e yayi godiya ga Alhaji....! Suka fita waje Inna tayi ciki itada rayyana shiko yatsaya" Alhaji suna fita yasaki shagaltaccen murmushi me ma'anoni dayawa.........

05
Shagaltaccen murmushi yasaki me ma'anoni dayawa' kuma daga Ranar muh'd yafara Aiki cikin jindad'i da walwala" satinsa biyu gidan yashiga rayuwar kowa kasancewar kyawawan d'abiunsa dayanayin nutsuwarsa da kamewa" qauna kuwa da gata bawanda Rayyana tarasa gurin muh'd komai yasamu bayawuce qanwarsa" wanda jama'a da dayawa kejin dama sune!

Wata safiyar lahadi misalin qarfe ukku na yammaci muh'd nagxaune kusa Da Alhaji yanamar tambayoyi A harkar makaranta yanabashi amsa" seyace muh'd xanmaidaku gidannan kaida innarku da qanwarka sabida Akwai wani business center daxanbud'e kuma kana d'aya daga wanda xasukance masukula da gurin...hawaye muh'd yaji sun wankemar fuska" cikin girmamawa yace Alhaji aikin dakabani kad'ai banida kalaman godiya Allah yasakamaka Da Aljannarsa!

Kan muh'd Alhaji yashafa tare dacewa kaina nayiwa karkaji komai domin ita duniya da kake gani Budurwa ce! Yau inada kud'i, mulki, qarshe kuma mutuwa xanyi so muh'd meye amfani mulki da dukiya inban taimaki maraya ba? dame xanyi tutiya gaban Allah inbanyi taimako ba"!? shiru muh'd yayi dagabisani yace Annabi yayi gaskiya dayace harduniya taqare ba'arasa naqwarai Alhaji mungode maganar dawowar mu nan da anbarshi karhidimar tayiyawa....!

Sake dafa muh'd yayi yace kai yarone baxaka gane ina nadosa ba! Kaga kana karatu sannan nace nad'auke ka aiki so fitar isha' xakayi kadawo shad'aya kaga behana ma karatu ba" kuma behanama aikin gida ba" kana hikimar dawoooo dakunan duniya talalace muh'd kanada qanwa macce da uwa wanda kaine jigonsu wani xaisama fitarka idooo yayi Abunda yagadama so hikimar dawo daku gidana kenan!!!

Shiru yayi aransa yace lallai Abunda babbah yahango yaro ko yahau tsani baxai hangoba...! Afili kuma kalmarnan yafurta da dukkk girman Alkhairi bakii bayawuce furtata! Idanuwansa nasauke qwallah rayuwarsa natunano Abbansu da dukkk kwanan duniya yakemar wasiya dukkk rintsi yaxama mai gaskiya gashi tun ba'aje koina ba yafara ganin "sakamako" dagabisani Alhaji yashige sashensa yabarshi da tunane tunane barkatai Axuciyarsa har lokacin tafiyarsu gida yayi! Tafiya suke kowa yayi shiru Rayyanah tace yah muh'd kayishiru" dubanta yayi cikin sakin fuska yace yau banajin surutu qanwata! Tab'e baki tayi tace ya Akejin surutu yah muh'd..!?

Dagashi har innah dariya sukasa sannan yanayi ciki ciki yace cewa kawai nayi ba'ajinshi dan hakan kawai xaifad'a taqyaleshi" but tasake jefo magana dacewa kuma kace koda wasa qarya haramun ne meyasa kaikayi..!? Sosa qeyarsa yayi yace inmunje gjda xanmaki bayani! Tace meyasa yanxu baxakamun ba!? Dariya yayi yace jarida xaimaki kyau Rayyana sedai naqallafa maraina law xakikaranta" innace tasako Baki dacewa Rayyanatu kam maaikaciyar Assibiti nakeso taxama"! Numfashi yasauke yace inna ko law yanada amfani ga y'amacce ni A tunanina in Akwai mata A harkar sharia xaasamu cigaba fiye da harkar lafiya!..cikin qarfafama d'anta tace meye hujjaraka!? Yace yauwa inna hujjata A harkar lafiya yanxu ansamu mata sosai Wanda suncike gib'in daakecewa y'ayamata su karanci lafiya"

Yakamata yanxu Asama harkar law gaba domin akaratun shima inkayi aiki akan gaskiya xaka ciribar Abunka xanbaki misali yadda wasu mutane kecin xarafin y'ayamata da ma maxa tahanyar miyagun kabairai (xina da luwad'i") shekaranjiya nagawani case A Court ankawo yaro d'anshekara ukku wani babban mutum yaybita bayanshi Duburar yaron nabada jini sosai" kuma ansani but sabida kud'i lauyan dake jan ragamar yaron se yabari akafishi points tohm ni A tunanina macce da tasan nauyin ciki daxafin Aihuwa baxata bari Ayaudareta dakud'iba! Tace gaskiyakam matar datasan xafin Aihuwa baxatayi wannan taadancin ba" yace yauwa innah shiyasa nake burin ganin Qanwata A matsayi Brr.

Dubansa tayi tace Allah yasanya taxama..Amin yace yana murmushi sukacigaba daxantawar har suka isa gida...suna jajintawa bayan sunkammala yagayama Inna saqon Alhajj" wani farinciki taji yaxiyarceta" Afili tace kai yakagani!? Cixa yatsansa yayi yace na'amunce nace kuma insha Allah sati mexuwa xamu koma! Tace shikenan Allah yasanya shine Alkhairi dama Ina fargabar biyan hayarnan Dan kud'in damukasa A banki baya biya" kuma Bashiru bayayimuna uxurin ko qwandala" murmushi yayi yace innah kibarma tuna lamarinsa komai yayi kansa duniyace"

Hakane ko muh'd je ka kwanta dare yayi kansa yasosa yace thommm bari nayiwa sarkin surutu Addu'a tayi bacci yamata yace seda safe" bayan gari yawaye sukayi gurin aiki kana kwanci tashi Asarar mebegen duniya yau sunkoma gidan Alhaji shikam baki har kunne haqarsa takusa cimma ruwa!!!!..

Haka dai suka soma sabuwar Rayuwa komai wadace harsunfara manata talauci kasancewar rayuwa da manta baya! Lokaci yajah sungama sakankancewa tare da miqa yardarsu ga Alhaji" sirrinsu bawanda sukeshayin gayamasa domin babuko shakka matsayin ubah sukabashi! Yau litanin dasanyin safiya Alhaji yashirya tafiya shida jabeer" Acewarsa xekaiwa tilon y'arsa Xiyara! Muh'd yakaisu harfilin jirgi amma bega tashinsu ba domin Alhaji yamatsamashi akan yaje gida yayi wasu Ayyuka" muh'd na'ajiye mota yayi school acanma besamu dawowaba se 6:pm yatsaya masallacin gidan yayi sallah wanda masu kud'i kenufi da Allah ga naka...!

Dagabisani yashigo yayikwance kanqafafun inna wanda rabonshi damata haka bayatuna Rana.."Inna kansa tadafa tace muh'd bakada lafiya ko..!? Kallonta yayi ido cikin idol yace Eh inna! Dayanayin damuwa tace jikinka babuxafi mekemaka ciwooo!? Seda yayatsina fuska yace gabana ke mugun fad'uwa tunjiya inna kimun Addu'a komenene xaixomin yaxo yadda hankalina xai iyad'auka" murmushi tayi tace komeye Alkhairine yace Allah yasa haka innarmu! Ina y'aruwata!? Dubansa tayi tace wanka take yace thomm Inna komai nakeyi yau ita nake tunawa Allha yasa Alkhairi ne! Amin tace xahiri" aranta kuma roqo take Allah karyaraba xumuntar bayinsa" suna wannan yanayin Rayyana tashigooo tayi face da itah..."Sedai bakamar yadda  tasaba ba shigowan ba' Inna da muh'd duka basuyi magana ba sunsan intaxauna xatafad'i damuwarta intanada"!!! sedai har mintuna goma suka shud'e batace komaiba, muh'd miqewa yayi Ahankali yace y'aruwata meke damunki..!? Dubansa tayi da xara xaran idonta se tayi shiruuuu! Hannunta yajah sukafita yana tambayarta cikin sigar rarrashi dakyar tayi murmushi tace bakomai" aransa yaji wani irri se yabasar kasancewar Rayyana batada xurfin ciki kuma batada qawa se yayanta.....lokaci yafara jahhhhh muh'd yaqitafiya seda inna tatakurashi sannan yatafi yakwanta bacci" solesalin dare yakama mutanen kirki kowa yad'auke sawuuuu Alhaji da jabeer sukayi parking mota tabaya suka shigo tafe suke Ahankali tamkar b'arayi suna matse jikinsu suna bin bangon daxaisadasu da sashen inna......

Daidai wannan lokaci kuma muh'd ya idarda sallah bacci b'arawo yasaceshi" rayyana nackin jikin innarta duk taduqunqune" inna kuma semishari take ba abunda ketashi se sautin fanka gidan yayi duhhhhu sosai sabida sunkashe fitillun lantarki

Novel start now!!

06
Gidan yayi duhu sosai sabida sunkashe fittillun lantark..." Kana Cikin wannan yanayin sukayi nasarar shiga d'akin batare da kowa yaji shigarsu ba se maliki yaumiddeen" jabeer yadanna 'eye Care light" Dan ganin inda xasuga rayyana! Cikin sa'a kuwa tana kwance da qaramar rigar baccin da tafito da brest d'inta, dayafara fita Aqirjinta Wanda ko inna batagama Ammanah shine kefita ba" bare muh'd!!!...murmushi yayi sannan yayiwa jabeer nuni dayakama innah yasamata igiya cikin magagin bacci taji hannun gardi naqoqarin rabata da y'arta!...

Araxane tamiqe xaune bakinta nafurta innalillahi wa Inna ilahim raji'un" kana batagama tantance  waye gabanta ba taji anma fuskartaa wani mugun d'auri da baqin qyalle Akajata akad'aure sannan Akasa mata plaster A baki" magana take bamaijinta se kakari" itako Rayyana se juyawa kawai tayi tana baccii Abunta" Alhaji murmushi yayi yace jabeer jamun qofa" murmushi yayi shima yafita yajah qufar Ahankali qiuuuuu...jabeer nafita kuma yayi miqahhhh yakoma xirrrr haihuwar u'warshi, yadinga takawa Ahankali harxuwa godon da rayyana kekwance"..

Hannunsa yad'ora Akan qafarta yanatafiyar tsutasa kad'an kad'an yanakai gurin brest d'inta tayi saurin dafewa tare da bud'e idonta" karaf sukayi ido cikin idooo da Alhaji" gigitacciyar raxana tabayyana Afuskarta tadubi yanayin da Alhaji keciki taduba ba innarta setafasa kuka yaxaro wuqa gefen gado yace kina motsawa xankasheki..." Cakkkk tahad'iye kukan tanadafe bakinta shikuma cikin xafin nama yarabata dakayan jikinta sauri tayi taburkice tamatse jikinta gammm murya qasa sosai takecewa A'ah A'ah A'ah A'ah tana girgixa kai! Amma ko Ajikinsa gurin yake neman xuwa!.

Ganin taqi bashi dama kokad'an yad'auketa ya Ajiye Aqasa yayi saurin Raba cinyoyinta gidabiyu yatura qafarsa taqasa yadda HQ d'in xaibud'e dakyau! Ya tsaya  yadubi gurin na seconds yad'ora harshensa! Yasauke Ajiyar xuciya sama samah" yadubi yadda penis d'inshi tawani miqewa yayi dariya yariqe rayyana qammmmm! Yanda take yawooo dajikinta yahanashi soka Abartashi cikin farjinta kana yana iya qoqarinsa taturo hannu tajahhhh penis d'in daqarfi amma besaduda ba seqara dafeta dayayi by mistake ta harba qafarta Sama tabugi y'ay'an marainansa dasauri yajah baya tamiqe tajah dabaya itama hannuta nakare da nonota tayi xufa kamar wadda akayiwa wanka dukkk gashinta yayamotse fuskarta gumi jagab da qwallah..!!!

Afusace yamiqe yasakeyowa kanta cikin muryar kuka datsoro tace menamaka..!?menamaka..!? Humm hummm menamaka..!?kaqyaleni Dan Allah karkarabani damutuncina naroqeka Dan Allah kajitsoron mahallicinka" idonsa jawur suka kad'a kana bemajin metake fad'a yasakeyowa kanta" dasauri tajuya tahad'a gabanta da gini tariqe sosai" qaramannata dabango yayi yatura penis d'inshi tabaya da Allah yaharamta" yana wani nishi sama sama yatura hannusa yadafe bakinta tsawon mintuna goma yana cacckata tabaya" qarfinta da muryarta sun disashe yajuyota tagaba yasake sakaw se qasss qassss gurin keyi tanajin wani masifaffen xafi dakyar takecewa! Innarmu kimun rai xekasheni yarabani dayayana" innarmu Dan Allah ki Agaje ni karnamutu nabar yah muh'd" tana wannan kukan da sanbato shed'ar ta tahange sabida Axabar ciwooooo!!!+ shikuma yagamsu yamiqe kodayatashi jini yawatsu inda yakeso! Gumi yawanke mishi yayi murmushi yasa kayanshi yafita"....

Yana fita jabeer yashigo shima dukkk Rashin imaninshi dayaga Rayyana seda yakauda kaiiiii gefe" kana yakwance inna yafita cikin xafin nama suka bi inda sukafito" Inna kuwa tanaganin Rayyana tasaki wani ihuuuuuuu me sautiiiiii da gigitar da xuciyar bil-adama" muh'd dagudu yayo cikin d'akin Axatonshi wutace!!! Kan yaqarso inna tarungumo y'arta taga yadda farjin yayi d'aid'ai se tajawo wuqa xatacaka dai dai xuciyarta muh'd yashigo! Be lura da rayyana ba yayo kan innah daxummar rabata DA wuqar amma beyi nasaraba Dan tasoka Aqirjinta yaxare wuqar yayijifa da itah yana wani mahaukacin kuka yace innarmu meyayi xafi haka...!? Hannunshi tariqo cikin magagin mutuwa tace muh'd kayi haquri idan y'aruwarka tah..tah...tatashi tagayama suwanene suka ketamata hadddi...? Araxane yadubi inda take nunawa yayi saurin runtse idonsa Dan haqiqa hankalinsa yakasa d'aukar Abunda idonsa suka  nunamashi......!

07
Yayi saurin runtse idonsa Dan haqiqa hankalinsa yakasa d'aukar Abunda idonasa suka nuna mashi.....! Atsorace yasake bud'e idonuwan kasancewar d'aya b'angaren xuciyarsa nashaida masa mummunan mafarki yake bagaskiya bane" mafison bayinsa baxai jarabceshi dawannan masifaba lokaci d'aya" yasake bud'ewa ganin kwatankwacin irrin nad'axu se yasaki wani ihuhhhhhh dayajawo hankalin jama'ar gidan yacire rigarsa yasanya Afarjin qanwarsa dake tsargin jini yana kuka med'aukar hankali daneman tarwatsewar eyes jikinsa nawani mugun xafi tamkar anahasa mashi wuta...." Kana yaqanqameta yana cewa wayene..!? Wayene!?humm Waye!? Hmmm" humm huhhh wayee!? waye!? Yana haka Su hajiya Rabi sukashigo toxali tayi da innar rayyana hawaye da jikinta sukafara kyarma atare, takasa furta komai se megadi ne yayi garfin halincewa mike faruwa!???? Daraxani muh'd yad'ago yace da megadi cikin qarajin kuka gayamu suwaye sukakashe innarmu tare da keta haddin y'aruwata yajaye rigarsa ga farjin rayyana" saurin rofe ido megadin yayi ganin yadda farjin yayi ragas se jini ke malala tamkar ankuna shawa sabida jijiyoyi daban daban ne ketsargo jini kasa furta komaiyayi se hawaye!  yanagirgixa kai axuciyarsa yace Allah sarki muh'd" domin Allah kad'ai yasan halin dayake ciki" Inna rabi datagani somewa tayi sabida tsabar tashin hankali...!

Sautin kuka yamamaye ko ta inahhhh" bamai sukunin rarrashin wani" can megadi yayinaxarin kiran police cikin sa'a bugu d'aya Akad'auka..yafara magana cikin muryar kuka yace kuna magana da gidan Alhaji tukur baraya" d'ansanda jinsunan Alhaji Tukur dasauri yace wane taimako xa'abada...!?! Nantake yabayyana masu mike tafiya gidan katsewa yayi dasauri yayi connection line da commissioner shima jin Gidan Alhaji tukur baraya yayi saurin miqewa" kasancewar wajibine Ayi tafiyar dashii' cikin mintuna suka had'a mota segidan kaitsaye sukayi ciki koda suka shigo gate  d'in farko megadi natsaye Ankunnah fitillun gidan tamkar rana....! Ahankali yayi gaba sukabi bayanshi" koda suka shiga Abun bakyan gani dayawa sunji rauni dukk dacewa irrin wannan ta'addancin ba sabo bane ga ganinsu!....

Kana commissinor yatambayi megadi ina Alhaji tukur...!? Yalabarta masa yatafi turkiya Asafiyar yau..! Duban megadin yayi yace Asafiyar yau...!? Yace Eh yallab'ai" shiru yayi kasancewar sunhad'u da Alhajin A gidan Gov....misalin 3pm today" kana Cikin Rauni commissioner yasanya hannunsa Aljihuuu yaxaro hankici yaduqa yad'auki wuqar da sukesa rankama kowaye sannan yajuyo yabama A.S.P yakub" daga bisani suka fara binciken gidan but Abun mamaki babu wata Alama menuni dacewa Anshigooo...!!!

Dukansu kansu yad'aure sedai bincike xainuna gaskiyar lamari amma commissinor aransa yakejin seyaga bayan kowaye yayi wannan ta'asar" sannan yace Asaka Inna mota da rayyana xuwa hospital" kafun safiya kana yace yana buqatar number da Akesamun Alhajin shiru megadin yayi kana yace nidai babu gurina sedai ga iyalinshi..yace inasuke!? Gatanan kwance yabada amsa" shidai muh'd yaxauce baya komai se kuka idonshi sunyi mugun jahhhhhhh" daxayabud'e mekallo yagani xaiyi ammannah hawayen jini xasu xubooooo"

Haka dai police d'in sukasa safar hannu" sukafara d'aukar innah muh'd be iyafurta kokalma d'ayaba, sabida girman baqin cikinsa yamunana! Sukadawo xuwa d'aukar rayyanah se yariqe gashi in xa'a xare ranshi besan menene Akeyiba..! suna magana yanagirgixa kai kana baraini bane besan meyakeyi ba sai da kyar sukarabashi da itah sukasata A'mota ganin yafad'i kwance yadaka goshinsa da cement A.S.P yakub yace yallab'ai muje dawannan kar Arasashi fa" yawuuu yahad'a yace ad'auko shi....haka suka tafi..."

Kaitsaye Accident and emergency police suka nufa cikin speciallist dake Sokoto" ba b'ata lokaci sukayi requsting amma ba anshesuba dole se Tatiary level haka sukajuya xuwa teaching hospital motar nataraira kuka weeeeee" weeee wuuuuuu" weee" anan kuwa suna xuwa aka amshesu itadai innah sashen bincike akawuce da itah Dan tabbatar da akwai rayuwar ko babu...!! Ita kuma Rayyana tiata room akashiga da ita kaitsaye kana qwararrun likitoci akan harka vagina sukashiga sedai kashhhh sunfirgita da lamarin sabida jijiyoyi masu wuyar lagetin dukkkk sun katseeeeee.........!

Sabida jijiyoyi masu wuyur lagetin dukkkk sun katseeeee.....


Amma wasu sukace xasu iya akabasau dama" suka fara neman jijiyar hannunta dan suyi coneccting N/S water..." but sukakasa samun jijiyar,,,"kana suka duba dukkkk wani place, da dokar lafiya tabada dama babu nasara!.

 Hakan yayi matuqar basu mamaki" kana wani daga cikinsu yabada shawarar Akira Doctor Hammah, masani akan Artery! bamusu group doctors d'in suka goyi baya! also Bab'ata lokaci Akakira hammah Cikin few minutes yaqaraso!... yanaxuwa yasaka hand globs Yaduba se yace A d'auketa daganan Akaita Hall theater.

 Bawandaa yayi magana akaturata xuwacan bayan sunshiga yace A d'orata Akan "tilting table" aka d'aureta kamar yadda ake buqata yamatso da theater light daidai farjin kana yad'auki wasu qananan qarafa da bature kekira (forceps) yasanyansu into her vagina suka rirriqe jijiyoyin dake tsargin jinin....! Kana yad'auko Rexer ya Aske gefen gashin kanta yashafa Kad'a da sprite sannan yasaka Allura akan jijiyar dayake tsammanin itace, cikin sa'a kuwa itace yasanya plaster! Sannan yasaki Ruwan yad'auko Fentazocin inj.... yayi off d'in ruwan yabata ita I.V slowly kana yad'auko nylon mexama nama cikin jikin mutum yafara d'aure jijiyoyin" bayan Awa d'aya yayi nasarar qarewa kana yasa kad'a yana share jinin dakuma tabbatarwa babu wani guri....!

 sannan yahau naxarin ta ina xefara had'e farjin.? anan kuma yagano ko ankimtsa farjin se ansaka mata robar fitsari kasancewar tasamu V.V.F (vesicovaginal fistula ) seyaga cewa tunda An datse jijiyoyin su kaita Ah Maryam Abacha women and children welfare V.V.F sokoto! Haka kuwa Akayi kasancewar can akwai qwarraru A surgery na masu fama da lalurar yoyon fitsari" police suka sake d'aukarta cikin Ambulance+ xuwa Assibitin haryanxu muh' d be iya magana ba sexafafan hawaye" xuciyarsa ma takasa karanto sunan Allah haka suka tafi emergency aka amshesu but Docton da yaduba yace Aiki bayauba setad'an farfad'o! Amma xa'a Ajeta "care of the parson room" nakwana biyu Agani" commissioner yace bakomai xasuje sudawo da muh'd sukatafi police sukayi sallah A.S.P yakub yacewa muh'd yana iyatuna yadda Abun yafaru!? Yayishiru! Yasake maimaita tambayarsa amma babu respond" gari kuma nawayewa Aka gayyaci y'an jareeda xuwa hospital Dan gayawa duniya Abunda yafaru! D'an jaridar ne yace yallab'ai tayaya xakuyi nasarar kama mutanen da ba'asan ko suwanene ba Duba da babuu wani shaida menuni dacewa Anshiga gidan Alhji tukur baraya!!!??????

 Shiru yayi sannan yace munada shaidar tambarin yatsu wanda akacakawa mahaifiyar yarinya kana ta itane xamubi qididdigar kowaye dan bima bayin Allahnan kadinsu kamar yadda shari'a ta tanada" wani d'an jareeda yajefo dacewa to binciken xefara ta inane ganin mutanen basuda Abokan Adawa yallab'ai!?

 Qwafa yayi sannan yace Allah xaitaimake mu mugane su yashige mota batare dabari sunqara tambayarsa ba" muh'd nasunkuye dakai sukayoooo cahhhhhh akanshi Amma bece komai ba se idanunsa ne ake d'auka Wanda su sukafassara yana Cikin *TSAKA MAI WUYA*..ganin ba'asamu bakinsa ba suka rankaya xuwa gidan Alhaji tukur kuma Adaidai wannan lokacin shafukan sada xumunta nayanar gixoooo natofa Albarkacin bakinsu"

Gefen Alhaji da jabeer misalin qarfe biyar nasafiya Agogon nigeria sukashiga turkia! Bayan gajeren hutu da yayi yakira y'arsa yace yashigo Turkia tun safiyar jiya yana *AMANRUYA* hotel" tsalle tayi sosai tace ganinan xuwa Abbana yaceee ok 003 hawa nabakwi suka katse line.....!  lauyan mekariya yamiqe yace yamai Shari'a Ina roqon wannan koto me Adalci da tayi duba dajawabanta nafarko tayi karatun tanatsu, takuma dubi Muhammad da Idanuwan baseera tayanke hukuncin Da hankali zaiiya d'auka...."kasancewar Ah cewar muh'd ceton mahaifiyarsa yayi yacire mata wuqa da ta cakawa kanta sabida tsananin Rud'u" wanda haryasa shacin faratansa bayyana" danhaka fitowar shacin hannunsa bataxama hujja daxa'aratye muh'd ba kasancewar babu shaidun gani daidooo ko d'aya" daqarfii wani yace Objection my low" Alqali yace qorafi bekarb'uba cigaba da maganarka..yagyara murya tareda kallon lauyan dayaso tsaidashi yad'ora dacewa Kuma shaidar da muh'd yadogara da itah ansaceta....."!

Rasss Rasss Zuciyar muh'd tabuga yace Ansaceta...!? Kowa seda yadubi muh'd kasancewar yadda yafurta kalmar tadasa qatuwar Alamar tambaya ga Zuciyar kowa...!? Yaruntse Idooo yace Innalillahi wa Inna ilaihim Rajiun ubangiji kanuna Isar mulkinka ga kosuwaye keneman tarwatsa Rayuwarmu" yajah baki yayi shiruuuu lauyan yacigaba dacewa..ba'ataba samun muh'd da laifin fyad'e kowani mugun haliba, Ah awan jininshi kuma yanuna baya tuammali damiyagun qwayoyj...kana Ah Binciken likitoci sunshaida mana cewa Penis d'inda tashiga jikin Rayyana muh'd" takai 18cm long haka kaurinta yakai 6-8cm" na muh'd kuma yana 14cm long kauri yatsaya Ah 3-4cm  Dan haka Ina roqon wannan koto me Adalci tawanke muh'd da zargin Da Akemasa har Izuwa Ranar da gaskiya zatayi halinta..." Shine bayyanar Rayyana wanda dukkk nisansa shekaru bazatamana fuskar da tashigeta ba" kana koto tayi hukunci da zaizama Izina ga kowanene!?

Gwaron numfashi Alqali yasaki kana yace lauyan mexargi nada Abuncewa...!? Shiru yayi Alamar babu" yasake mamaita lauyan mezargi nada Abuncewa..!? Jin shiru yagyara xaman gilashinsa Yace bisa sauraren hujjoji masu qarfi ga mekare wanda Akexargi" wannan koto me Adalci tasallami muh'd muh'd bisa xargin da Akemashi nafyad'e ga qanwarsa dakuma kisan gillah ga mahaifiyarsa...bisa dogaro da hujjoji wanda sukayi Dai dai da Sashen "A" sakin layi na tara cikin baka tahud'u.........00000000

Alqali nafita mutane sukayo kan muh'd sunajajinta lamarin,,,, y'anjarida sukatambayeshi yaya yakejin hukuncin da Alqali yayanke masa...!? Dakuma Irrin farincikin dayakeji!?

 Seda yashafe qwallah yace dabadan kar ubangijina ya zargeni da butulciba da nace Sam banafarinciki....." Yaqara shafe qwallah karo nabiyu yace banida kowa yanxu se Allah...." Dakuma salmah datake sona pisabilillah" wani d'an jarida yace muh'd bakada dangi ubane kona u'wah..!? Da harkake iqirarin bakada kowa!?

Kuka yaqara jahn yace inada Dangin Ubah Amma banida Alaqa dasu sabida su mabiya "ISA" Alaihi salam ne' nikuma babana musulunci yakeso" humm haaa hummmm hahh wanda hakan yasashi qaura Zuwa garin Sokoto cikin "Yabo local Government" yasamu yakarb'i Shahada gurin Sheak Ibrahim Ibrahim" yacanxa Suna daga "lekan" xuwa Muh'd Akabashi Auren innata" dayake qauyene gori yaqi qarewa" suna nunamasa qyama sunaganin sune masu Addini...babu yadda ya iya yanason musulunci dole yadawo cikin gari yana Rayuwarsa har Akahaife mu ina 10yrs Rayyana nada shekara d'aya innatace seyakaita danginsa tagani....."

Yana nunamata kasheshi xasuyi tanace Yasanya mu gaba se  Abekuta" wanda tunshigarmu bamuji me Gigin Furta sunan Allah ba" bare yamiqe zuwa sallah... Haka har muka isah "Court Road" unguwa  me kyau da tsari shiru bamotsinkowa" mukakai gaban wani gida baba yace nashine shiyagina Amma yabarshi sabida musulunci, inna tasha mamaki ganin Daula da Dukiyar da babanmu yabari yadawo musulunci" yashiga cikin gidan Babanshi cikin tsawa yace yafita bai fitaba sukafara jifarmu da Dukkk Abunda sukasama" dole Inna tagoya Rayyana babana yagoyani muna guduuu dakyar mukatsira" bayan mundawo kwana biyu ciwon ciki yakashe babana" tundaga Ranar nafara koyon d'abiun manya dason zamewa nagari tare dason kud'in daxanjiyarda Rayuwa biyu dad'i Amma  Qaddara ta tarwatsa komai...Shiyasa nakejin banida sauran farinciki dayawuce Mutuwa...."!

Dayawa seda imani yaratsa su" kana bawanda yasake cewa komai salmah tajawo hannun muhd suka nufi mota" jabeer seda yaji kamar yahad'e Zuciya yamutu sabida tsabar ciwwooooo" Alhaji ma yaji haushi amma yanuna farincikinsa dole haka suka tada mota Ixuwa gida.........

32
Suka tada mota Zuwa gida..Acikin motar ne Alhaji yace muh'd kaga lamarin Allah ko...!? Afirgice yace Alhajin mune nakeji ko kamanceceniyar muryace!? 

Gyara zama Alhaji yayi kana  yace nine dai naku muh'd...Shiru yayi zuciyarsa nacemasa kardai shine mahaifin Salmah!? Alhaji yakatse shirun dacewa mamaki kake ko!? Muh'd salmah y'atace" maimakon magana se Zarata hawaye Ga Fuskar muh'd shaaaaaaa....

Salmah kamar zatayi kuka tace shaihi kaikace mun kayitawakkali! meyasa kakewa qaddarar ka kuka"!? bayan kasani manyan Bayan Allah masu qasqantar da kai da haquri Ubangiji kejarbta domin yazama Wa'azi ga bijararru Asararru" wanda basa Aikata Alkhaikari se sharri" da irrin wannan qaddarori ne Ubangiji ke nuna ikonsa" insunyi sa'a sugane inbasuyi ba hawuya ko jahannama taxame makomar Rayuwarsu..wanda yajarbta insukayi Immani yadauwamma ni'ima dajin dad'i daniima garesu Ranar da babuwata Rahama seta ubangiji...Wanda kuma haqurinku ko naui'in d'aci baxaibaku wannan Rahamar ba..sedan ubngiji metausayin bayine yasaka wanda sukayi Imani....jabeer yagama qulewa hakama Alhaji jiyake kamar yacewa Salmah ta dakata.

Shikuma Yana Rishin kuka yace ba Qaddarata nakewa kukaba....banason sake maimaita Rayuwa gidan salmah" domin In ina numfashi ciki kullum baxansamu nutsuwa ba....." Seda tasadda kai qasa tace Ah ah "shaihi" karkayane hukuncin haka...domin inkabar gidanmu bansan Ina zaka Dosa ba"  Shiru yakasa furta komai" se salmah ce tace Jabeer Hospital zakawuce damu Aduba lafiyar Idonshi" karaf yace Ah Ah banabuqatr sake ganin komai Aduniya" sabida me!? Uhmm salmah rashin ganina watahikimace daga Allah SWA mubarshi yawanzar da ikonsa" zayi magana yace karkinace da matsamun domin baxanjeba....shiru yaratsa kowa dasaqar Zuciyarsa harsuka Isa Gida....

Bayan sunyi Parking Sukafito Salmah tariqo hannun muh'd sukayi ciki, jabeer yasakejin tsanar jabir Da Danasani Numfashinsa Adorn qasa...." Haka shima yabisu cikin Gidan" Bayan sun huta hajia tayiwa muh'd jaje tare da nasihohi masu Ratsa jikin irrin nwanda sukayi Imani Da Allah" taqarfafa masa Allah gwanin hikima da baseera ne" shiyasan dalilinsa nayihaka" kana dasannu zaiwarware komai yafauwallah masa lamarinsa..! Har Aransa yaji dad'i kana yace ngd hajia da tinatarwa Allah yaqara gabaci  tace Amin...tareda gabatar masu Special launching" ba laifi muh'd yad'anci da qarfafawa Salmah....,

Haka lokaci yatafi misalin 2:00pm sukayi sallah jabeer yawuce gida yanata saqa yadda za'ayi yakawar da Muh'd tarihinsa yashafe Azukatan Al'ummh domin tsanarsa dayakeji inyabarshi Da Rayuwa Zunubine da Idanuwansa baxasu yafe mai ba...haka ya isha Gida"... muh'd kuma xaune yake bimiqe daga inda yayi sallar ba yanata gayawa Allah damuwarshi..ganin haka salma tajahhhh mota batazame ko inaba se @Al~XEEYAN plaza dake Gobirawa" cikin garin Sokoto....

Gurine nasiyarda d'inkakku kayan maza da namata kama nagargajiya dana y'angayu masu tashen qarya" tashiga b'angaren hagu tafara xab'en Shaddodi masu kyau datsari da Akaqawata dad'inkunan yayi...Akayi lissafi tabiya takoma b"angaren gabas shikuma d'akine da Akayi jeren tulaluka y'an Barno, saudi Arabeia da sauran qasashe Duniya....tasiya tabiya tafito bayan Yaron shop d'inyasaka mata kaya Amota ta tada tawuce koda tashigo muh'd naxaune kan sallayar da tabarshi"

Tashiga b'angaren da taroqi Dad Abawa Shaihi taga Angyara kamar yadda ta buqata" amma dukkk da haka seda tasake gyara gadoo taqara Zuba tulale dayake tasan Muh'd maabuci Son qamshi ne takunnah "AC" sannan ta Fito tad'auko kaya datisiya masa tamasu maxauni" komai lafiya lau taxxo tasameshi tay ixaune tagefe" tadad'e Ahaka sannan yace kitashi kishiga ciki salmah...murmushi tasaki kana tace dama b'angarenka xannuna mah" bamasu yace thom muje haka sukajera har cikin d'akin tamaijagora yaxauna kan Gadooo" sannan itama taxauna bayan wucewar wasu daqiqu yace ngd salmah...cikin murya mesanyi da laud'i tace shaihi yiwa kaine......

Yad'anyi yaqe tareda yin shiru salma takatse dacewa Shaihi tashi kayiwanka kacanjah kaya" Seda yajah mintuna yace barni haka salmah..qasa tayi da  Murya sosai tace Dan Allah shaihi kayiwanka, kasan Ko bakomai Annaxafatu minal imani...sannan Allah tsalkakkene bayakarb'ar Abu se metsalki Dan Allah katashi kajiiiii....

yadda ta marairaice murya har dariya tasoma bashi" yace thom innayiwanka Inakayan da zansaka salmah..!? Gabaki d'aya tarungumo kayan tace gasu" hawaye suka gangaro wanda yarasa ko namenene".

Yace Sadani da toilet amma basai kintab'ani ba kasancewar haramunne" baki tabud'e tamkar yana ganinta sannan tace thom yazaayi kagane shaihi..!? Seda ya murmusa yace kiyi gaba senabi baya" inkikashiga kikunnah shawa kikashe Zangane" haka kuwa Akayi harxatafita yace bani kayan da zansaka innayi wanka...bamusu tafitooo tabashi" yarufe yasaki shawa ga kansa hawayen dayake tariritawa kar salmah tagane suka goceZurrr zurrr..seda yacikuka sannan ya yafara wanka sama sama yashirya yafitooo!!!! Salmah cikin zumud'i tace masha Allah shaihi kayi kyau!!!

 Ngd kalmar da yafurta kenan..haka lokaci yatafi salmah bata fita d'akin muh'd ba Se 11:00pm koshi seda yanuna b'acin Ransa" tana fita yatada Kai Sama yace Allah sarki Duniya meraba qauna" tayanke Zumunci Anshirya ko ba'a shirya ba....."itakuma bayan tashiga cikin gidan toilet tafad'a tayi wanka tsalki tafitooo tana tsefe kanta hajia Rabi tashigo da sallamarta, da fara'a salma ta Amsa tace hajiata...zaunawa tayi tace tund'axu nakeyawo kinacan d'akin muh'd" Ido taxurawa hajia Rabi tareda tsaida Tsifar kanta Dan Dukkk lokacin da hajia ta nutsu Irrin haka thom xatayi magana me muhimmacci! Zargin salma kumatabbata....

Dan cikin Sigar hikima tace salmah yanxu ke bayarinya bace da xannunwa dai dai. Kinsamu ilmi Addini dai dai gwargwado" kinasamu Nazamani" amma duk dahaka zanja hankalinki da tunatarwa Akan Burin mahaifinki na Auren jabeer....karki shagala  da zuciya kasancewarta mugun nama watarana dan nasan tanagayamaki shaihi zaki Aura"

Cakkkkk salmah taji komai yatsaya" dataimakon Allah tadawo gaban mahaifiyarta tace Hajiya Dukk duniya in Akwai Abunda banason ji kogani Bayawuce jabeer" natsaneshi tsanar da taharamta d'an Adam yayiwa koda kare, bare jinsinsa! 

Innillahi salmah karkibani kunya Akun tutiya danakeyi dake..."  hannun hajiya salma takama tace meye Ribarku inkuntirsasani Auren Dole!? Meyasa baxakubarni da zab'inaba...seda ta kwantar da murya tace Salmah Auren dole bashine silar tabbatuwar qiyayyah ba...hakama Auren so bashikenuni dacewa xa'agina Rayuwa me ma'ana ba" sa'a naga Allah kicigaba daneman zab'inshi, kasancewar dayawa Abunda kakeso kanzama Sanadin Qunci gareka....wanda kake tsananin qiyayya yakasance Alkhairi gareka!!!! Shiru salmah tayi kana tayiwa hajia Alqawarin xata nemi zab'in Alllah.....'

Tun daga Wannan  Dare Rayuwa taci Gaba hartsawon watanni shidda" Muh'd da salmah sunyi shaquwa taban Al'ajabi, wanda har d'aya  baya jin farinciki se yaga D'aya" Salmah dukkk wani labari daxaikwantar da hankalin muh'd shine takeyi dan ganin tatsaida kukan Shaihi dayaxame masa tamkar Ciwooo...!

 gefen Rayyana taji Sauqi harsundawo Gida Nigeria" kana hajia Asma'u nakula da Itah fiye da tunani me tunani....yau dasanyi safiya Rayyana naxaune tana Tuna Abubuwan dasukafaru se hawaye sha batare da sautin kukaba..... Hajia Asma'u Razane tayo kanta wanda hakan baqaramin takaici yaba muhusin dake xaune yana Zanen kalar gidan dayakeso..."talura dayanayi d'anta Amma tabasar tayo kanta tarungumota jikinta tanashafa Ahankali tunda batada Ilmin magana da Kurma, suna wannan yanayi Abban muhusin yashigo shima Araxane yace Maman muhusin meyasameta...!?

Kamar zatayi kuka tace wlh bansani ba" hannunta yariqo shima yasakata jikinsa kana yarasa me xaice mata tunda magana Da kurma ma ilmine mexaman kansa" yadubi Hajia Asma'u yace thomm yanxu yazamuyi da itah....!? Kantayi magana muhusin yad'ago yace yaxaayi ko Akaita makarantar musakai dake sokoto..

Shiru Iyayen sukayi kana Abban nashi yace kakawo shawara Amma bayanxuba se taqara shan magani munkoma mombai sunga yanayin lafiyarta se nafara nema mata Gurbin karatu Ah can sedai baxanso boarding ba nafison Day" mihusin dai bece uppan ba Asmau tace hakan yayi dai dai" haka Rayuwar ketafiya kowane b'angare" yau dasanyin Safiya muh'd nazaune bakin qofa jabeer yadanno mota qirrrrrrrrrrrrrr gigice yamiqe jin mota tayo cikinsa" samm jabeer baji dad'in Rashin Saarsa ba Amma Afili yadafa muh'd yace kayi haquri Motar taqwace mun..." Murmushi kad'ai yayi tareda furta babu komai...suka had'a hannu yayi ciki kansa tsaye cikin Sa'a Alhajin naciki suka gaisa yaxauna d'aure da fuska..."

Alhaji yagane mejabir kenufi Amma yabasar dacewa jabeer lafiya..!? Seda yasosa qeya yace maganar Aurena kace najira zuwa watan dayagabata gashi har wani watan yashigo yakai 3 banji wani labariba...seda ya daidaita zamansa kan kujera yace Qara mun wata d'aya jabeer zansan Abunyi..

33
Yace Qara mun Wata d'aya jabeer zansan Abunyi....gwaron numfashi jabeer yasauke yace Shikenan Amma inbakad'au matakiba ni zan d'auka" karkadamu kanka Aure kamar Anyi angama ne sukayi sallama yatafi"..

Bayan yafita Alhaji yamiqe yana Zagaya falo tareda laluben mafita" amma ransa motsi kawaiyake besamu mafitarba. Dole Yaxo yasake xaunawa tsayin mintuna Zuciyarsa tabijiro masa  da sabon Al'amari wanda Afili yace baxaiyuyuba kashe "Muhammad" sabida hakan zaisa Ayi binciken da komai Zai lalace..." yana Wannan sambatu hajia Rabi tashigo d'auke da trey wanda ke d'auke da zallar Fruits.

 saurin saitawa yayi yace kamar kinsan Inajin yunwa" murmushi tasaki Taxauna kana tace Alhaji meyake Faruwane kake b'oye min? Cikin mamaki yace bangane tambayarki ba hajiya....

Qara daidaita Mazaunanta tayi tace nashigo kana sambatu amma kabasar dacewa wai kanajin yunwa...niko naga yanxu nakai wani mizani dabaikamata kab'oye mun Wani Abuba, dukk dacewa dayawanku maza kuntafi Akan Ra'ayin d'aya, shine Duuk girman macce ba'ad'aukar maganarta Wanda hakan babban kuskurene" wataqila inkafad'i damuwarka tahango kai Abunda baka hangoba kasancewarta u'wa me rainon jinsi Biyu....

Sokoko yayi, sannan yanisa yace gaskiyane hajiya..kana sambatun danake bewuce kunyatar dani da salmah keburin yiba...domin dayawa zasukalleni maci Amanar zamatare" wasu sukalleni me son Zuciya! Nikuma nakalli kaina Uban da baya Iya Alqalancin gidansa hajiya wannan shine Damuwata....!

Seda tanisa tace dukkk wannan barakataccen tunani Kawai domin yanazuwammaka ne daga Shaid'an La'ananne Allah" Alhaji nida zakabinawa kaima kakafe harshenka Akan Addu'a kasancewar itace makamin musulmin kirki, karkanace cewa seta Auri jabeer domin wataqil ba Alkhairi bane' sabida salma kwata kwata batasan jabeer, ta tsaneshi tsana maimuni" kuma sanin kankane ba'a raina qiyayya dukk qanqantarta domin komai na Iyafaruwa A koma Danasani......"

Tabbasss hakane hajia amma koda bata Auri jabeer ba bazanbari ta Auri Muhammad ba...!

 Cikin sauri  hajiya tace Sabida me Alhaji...!?

Sabida dalilai dayawa" tooooo kamar me kenan!?

Nafarko bashida Asali" matsiyaci ne" nakasashe" wani Abun tozarci ne gareni yakasance sirikina" mijjn y'ata danafiso sama darayuwata" jikokina sufito tsatson kafurci Abune wanda baxantab'a goyon bayaba....

Karkace haka Alhaji....daqarfi yace kenan ke kina goyon baya....!? Seda tayi qasa damurya tace Ah ah wlh bahakna nake nufi...to yakike nufi..!? Kayi haquri Wlh harga Allah bansan maganar zatab'ata maka rai ba dana nisanta harshena da furtawa kasancewar Aljannata nake nema gurinka" kuma dalilna na furta haka nakaranta hadisai daban daban Da masubada labari suka jiyo dagabakin Babban jekadan Allah. Majidad'in Allah Annabi muh'd (SAW) yana cewa idan d'ayanku yaxo neman Aure kukayarda da Addininshi to kubashi, Inbahaka ba fitina zatabiyu baya Agareku wanda Inkayi nazari Annabi muh'd yayi gaskiya kadubi yadda Rayuwar Aure ta tab'arb'are cikin Al'ummah, Zawarawa gasunan inkana lissafi kanka zaikwance" bugu daqari zinace zinace da matan Aure da mazan Aure kiyi wanda hakan yasa dayawa narenon y'ay'an wasu Anasu..."

Afusace yakatseta dacewa shiyaxo Neman Auren ne Akahanashi Eh malama!???? Sadda kai qasa tayi tace bexoba Amma nuni ya ishi me hankali" thom tunda ya ishi me hankali ki Aura matashi ni seyanxu ma nagano inda kikadosa!!! amma kisani in kikazama Silar Raba Aurennan Wlh se nasab'a maki naquntata maki yaficeee fuuuuuhhhh

Murmushin takaici hajia Rabi tasaki" kana tace Ikon Allah daga magana se Abu yaxama fitina"!???? Tasake yin Zugummmmm tana haka salmah tashigo" kana taji jikinta yasanyaye ganin tanata rafka sallamah hajiya bata Amsaba...

Tatako kamar wadda qwai yafashewa Aciki taduqa tare da xunguro hajiarta...firgigit tadawooo daga Duniyar da talula sedai batace komaiba....

Cikin Rawar murya salmah tace hajia lafiya...!?

Inafa lafiya salmah tunda kinfison muyita fad'awa cikin Rud'ani...A tsorace tace bangane fad'awa cikin Rud'aniba hajiya...!?kiyimun bayani yadda xangane" wane ganewa zakiyi salmah bayan Kinsan Burin mahaifinki Amma kike kawar dakai kina runtse Idooo waike dawanda kike so....

Cixa leb'e salmah tayi tace hajiya Dan nace banason jabeer kikace haka..!? Hajiya Ashe laifine d'a yasanar da iyayensa bayason Abunda sukeso!? Balaifi bane In dah baida mutunci ga Idonsa tamiqe tabar salmah zaune Amma Azuciyarta tasan nababba dayaro ne takewa y'arta!!!!!!

Irrin wannn kalaman ne ke takai kawo A qwaqwalwarta" Wanda sedatakwashi Kusan mintuna Arba'in da biyar sannan Zumbur tamiqe tayi kitchen kamar xautacciya" kaitsaye tad'ibi Abunci tanufi hanyar fita" bataxame Ko inaba se d'akin muh'd..

xaune yake rungume da Filo tace Muh'd kayi haquri wlh samm mantawa nayi.. Nisa yayi yace Banga laifinkiba salmah kasancewar Yau da gobe se Allah" kana dukkk Rikicin da Ake ciki inasane".....

Rasss! Rass! gabanta yafad'i kana kamar me shirin kuka tace Dan Allah shaihi karkabarni" rikicin da Akecikin Gida narantse xan iya tsaida komai" dukkk daba yaganinta yaji tausayinta matuqa, kana cikin lafazi me taushi yace salmah banmaki Alqawarin bazanbarki ba.....kasancewar In bancika ba Allah zaitambaye ni dalili....

Hannaye biyu tasa tatoshe bakinta karsautin kukan dayaxo bashiri yafita still tana wannan yanayin tace shaihi, haryanxu kanada kwakwanto Akan son danake mane!? Ko kuma dagaske bakasona kamar yadda zuciyata ke gangancin fad'amun ina Qaryatata...!?

Shima kamar yayi kukan yana girgixa kai yace Ah Ah salmah, karkice haka Amma tun bayauba nasani banyi daidai da mijin daxaki Aura ba" Ido taware tace Shaihi sabida mahaifina nada kud'i kake ganin Rashin dai daiton mu!? Ko kuma dai tarbiyata ce batamaka ba!?

Shiru yayi be Amsaba taduqa tace Shaihi gani gabanka Ina roqon Abunda kud'i,ilmi,Asali, kyau, basa gayyatarsa se Allah ya hallitasa Axuciya" humm hum Shaihi Dan Allah kace kana sona" koda ba harcikin Zuciyarka bane" seda yasaita ciwoooon dayakejj yace salma bason kine banayi ba" sedai jabeer yafni cikar zati Asali ke dakomai mah...

Uhmmmm kayikuskure in haka zuciyar ka kasaqawa domin So besan cikar xati ko Asali ba Amma naqara roqonka karo nabiyu, Shaihi kataimaki Rayuwata da tashiga tsananin Soyayyarka" katausayawa xuciyata badan niba sedan Wanda ke hallitr so cikin Zuciyar da batasan hawaba batasan sauka ba..shaihi Ina sonka fiye da yadda xuciya ke buqatar jini daganin ta watatsashi ga Sassan jiki...

Tausayinta yasake fisgarshi yace Salma Ina sonki batun yauba' Amma Ina nan kanbakana banyi Alqawarin Aureba kasancewar Burina yanxu bewuce ganin Rayyanatu ba" dayawa takanxo Amfarkina tace muh'd kaqara jinkiri Ina Kan hanya kinga kuwa kowace safiya ita nake dakon shigowarta" hallau mafarkina gaskiyane ko qarya xanjira harsnaga Ranar ko kuma in rai yayi halinsa" kinga inxakiyimin Adalci banida lokaci rainon soyayya" kuma ke maccece bakida lokaci tamkar ni" kana bankasance meso zuciya ba da senace ki jirani...Salmah kiyi haquri in nafad'a maki ba daidai ba.....

Cikin nutsuwa tace ko badaidai bane bazangane ba shaihi komai ka Akaita jikina kanbani daidaine" amma in kayimun Alqawarin Aure zanjiraka Shaihiiii" bakinsa yabud'e yace shikenan Salma namaki Alqawrin Aure bayan bayyanar Rayyanatu" wata qaramar qara tasaki tare dacewa *I love You Allah* daka bani yardar Shaii" murmushi Muhammad yasaki Dan yadda salma tayi maganar tabashi dariya sedai beyiba Dan yaharamtawa kansa har se Ranar da Rayyana tabayyana gareahi... Kana salmh tamiqe ta d'ora Alwalah dan nuna jin dad'inta ga Allah bad'ini...haka lokaci yatafi!

Kana tundaga yau Salmah tafara Rayuwar qunci tsakaninta da Iyayenta har tsawon sati biyu bamaice mata uppan Sedai takanmanta batadashiri dasu dukkk tatuna tasami Shaihiii...yau jumu'a bayan tasowa masallaci Alhaji yakira jabeer gidansa na Cikin gari Dake Rumbukawa...bayan sun tsotsa lemu Alhaji yace jabeer nayi iya qoqarina salmah tabani had'inkai ta bujire, Inaso kafad'i daga naira har dala nawa kakeso nabaka kajanye Auren salmah....

Xumbur jabeer yamiqe yace Alhaji bamuyi hakaba ba Wlh son da nakewa salmah yafi gaban Afansheshi da kud'i shekara Goma Ina jiran takasance matata" kana kamun Alqawarin tanaso ko bataso zaka mallaka mun itah narufe ma Asiri...." Naji nagani nagani na binne Amma yanxu kabijiro dawani xance!? karka karye mun Alqawari domin wlh nima baxan kalli Amanar dake tsakaninmu ba zanbankad'o Asirin da kabinne Uwarka da ranta kafun kasamu shigowa siyasa...ga kuma sabo shine fyad'e ga qanwar wanda kakeso kabawa y'artaka kuma ina da shaidar da xa'ayadda dani.....

Jirine yakaryo ma Alhaji kasancewar In Akwai sirrin dabayason Atunamashi shine binne uwarsa da yayi daranta wanda Aka kunna wuta Akace Gobara tacinyeta....kana Agaggauce yace dakata Jabeer xansaka bikin da muh'd Amma xa'ad'aura Auren dakai....wani shagaltaccen kallo yayiwa Alhajin kana yace kanaganin mafitace .!?

Seda yashare Gumi yace thom nawa Akayi kuma Aka xauna lafiya..!? Ohk shikenan Akafta dahaka suka rabuuu" Alhaji tausayin Allah yakawoshi Gida lafiya kanayana fitowa mota yayi falon baqi yafara saqa Abubuwa game da jabeer wata zuciyar ihuuuuu take yasanya Akasheshi yahuta" wata Zuciyar tace yakama kansa Dan jabeer yafi Virus Rascalism..." ....dole yagoya hannaye bayansa yana Neman mafitah.....

Gefen Rayyana kuma cikin tausayin Allah da amuncewarsa Ba"acewa komai dan tasamu sauqi sosai kana komai takeso shi Akemata sedai qwaqwalwarta takasa mance Ranar da Alhaji yashigeta da yah muh'd da kuma Rumfarta wato Innah, sune kesanya tana Zubda hawaye" kana Ansama mata gurbin karatu Ah Abdul-Rashid Adisa raji special school dake Sokoto wanda zatafara karatu Ranar Monday da yardar Allah.....haka b'an garen ketafiya

Alhaji kuwa bayan yasaita yakira muh'd dan Shaidamasa qudirinsa Amma bud'ar bakinsa yafad'i maganar dataso tsaida numfashin Alhaji....

Yafad'i maganar dataso Tsaida numfashin Alhaji....Kana cikin Raxani Yace Muhammad kasan Abunda kace dani kuwa!????

Yace Eh nafad'amata Aure bayanxuba senaga y'aruwata.." Alhaji numfaahi yasauke menauyi jin bakuskure maganar dai itace muh'd yasake nanatawa... Also yasaita yace Muhammad Kantsu kadawo hankalinka Amma yanxu inazakaga Rayyanatu!? Da harzakace kaida Aure seka ganta..!?

Kansa yasadda Qasa..yace Rayyanatu batanesa, batakusa domin takanxo Cikin baccina tashaida mun tananan Dawoowa gareni Injirata....

Kaiiii Alhaji yajinjina yace nagane tashi kayi tafiyarka" bayan yafita yakira hajia Rabi yace nakira Muhammad maganar Auren salmah Amma Akwai matsala.....!

Matsala kuma Alhji....!? Eh dan nahagi yanafama da tab'uwar qwaqwalwa....daqarfi tadafa Qirji tace Innalillahi wa inna ilaihim Rajiun Tab'uwar qwaqwalwa kumaa?

Eh sabida naga Almar Schizophrenia tare dashi!? Shin Schizophrenia...!? Wlh tallahi hajiya kinsan sune kefama da hallucination (hearing Voice) kuma Abun bagaskiya bane" Zuruuuu tayi kana tace se Agaggauta kaishi Psychiatric hospital tunda Allah yasanya mungane dawuri yace shiyakamata muyi gaskiya...Amma Duk dahaka zantuntub'i Dr. Abdul qahar inji Shawararsa..kana sukayi shiru kowa nasaqa Abunda yadameshi...Dagabisani Alhaji yafita yanata tunani barkatai....

Gefen su Rayyana kuma sosai Akayimata sayayyar makaranta Alhaji da hajia Asma'u muhusin gabaki d'aya sukayomta Rakiya" tafiya suke hankali kwance Cikin "406" blue Anawuce Daxuxxukan da suka Raba zamfara da Sokoto....cikin Ikon Allah misalin 1:00pm sukashigo Birnin Shehu" tsarin garin da nutsuwarsa da kamewar mutanen da Ala'adarsu yaburge Alhaji matuqa Dan Rabonshi da sokoto tun Raba kebbi, zamfara sokoto kowane gari yazauni State.kana yanata mamakin cigaban da sukasamu har Suka Isa Gate D'in A.A RAJI Special school....yataka birki yatsaya.

Wani jami'in tsaro yataso yabincike komai sannan yace sushiga....sunyi d'an tafiya Akasake tsaidasu nanma dai binciken ne dagabisani Aka nunamishi Gurin parking" yabi kwanar yayi sukafito" kowanne tsaye yayi yanakallon tsarin makarantar dayaso dai dai da naturai" kana dayake sunyi Martaba Biru suka d'aga kai sukakaranta Wani Baqin Rubutu Da'akayi da manyan kalmomi...." Ahankali sukacira qafafunsu sukayi Wani b'angare da Akarubutawa "Care of the parson with special need"

Maza da mata ne matasa da magidanta Zazzaune Kana Dukansu kurame ne, da makafi, da guragu! sukayi sallama cikin harshe larabci Aka Amsa" Alhaji yashiga yabasu hannu muhusin ma yana qyama qyama yamiqa masu hannun..." Bayan sungaisa Alhaji yace yanason ganin shugaban makaranta" Wani makahone yayi Amfani da hannayensa yace dawani kurma yatashi yakaisu d'akin Shugaba...."

Miqewa yayi Akace subi bayanshi..tunda kurman yafito yaji gabansa yayimugun fad'uwa sakamakon toxali da yayi da Rayyana" dasauri yasadda kansa qasa yayi murmushi yawuce..gaba fuskar rayyana natamasa Gezau Amma besan mehakan kenufi ba!? Dan baitab'aji ba seyau..

Yashiga da faratansa yagaida shugaban kana yagayamasa gamasu nemansa" shima da faratan yace kace sushigo.. Fuskarsa yaxuro yamasu Alamar shigowa idonsa naduba Rayyana..itakam bataluraba har sukashiga " Abban muhuseen yagaisa da shugaban haka muhusin shima yagaidashi kana suka zauna tareda miqa masa wata farar takarda...

Bud'awa yayi yaduba yay'ago yace Alhajj yabakasa Sunan yarinyarba..!? 

Murmushi yayi hajia tayi karaf tace Sa Rayyanatu muh'd warure" mantawa mukayi" yace ohk tare da rubutawa! Daga bisani yace Zamukaita Islam House" kuma zatafara karatun ta daga matakin farko dukk da tawuce munzili, nakarb'i uzurinka cewa daga baya tasamu qaddarar godiya yayi Ainun..kana Shugabn yakira Wani gurgu yace yakira mai Marwan ba jjmawa sega kurmannan yazo yace gashi...." Bayani yayimai da hannu cewa Akai Rayyana Islam house" murmushi yasaki kana yamata magana Amma batagane ba tadaiga yanayawo da faratansa...

Yakalli shugaban yace bata iya Sing language bane!? Yace baquwa ce kana yamasa bayaninta da qaddarar datasameta" kai yagirgixa yab'ata Rai Alamar beji dad'i ba, kana yaroqi Allah yabata haquri" Shugaban Ya Amsa da Amin kasancewar shikad'ai ke fahimtar yaren...kana marwan yace tazosuje..

Matsawa tayi jikin Asma'u Alamar bazatajeba! Alhaji da hajiya basuce komai.

shugaban yayi dariya yace tayi boko..!? Da kamar Abban muhussin yace A'a sekuma yace Eh tafara" ya yaga ferar takarda yayi Rubutu da harshen Bature cewa yasaunanki !? Yabata harda biro!

Ahankali Taduqa Cikin qayataccen Rubutu Ta Rubuta Rayyanatu muh'd...da Alhaji da Asma'u seda sukayi musayar idooo kasancewar tambaya datamasu dirar mikiya kodai tanaji!? Suka tsuke baki shugaban yaduba yaqara "warure" yarubuta mata cewa kiriqa cikawa da warure inxaki Rubuta kinji Rayyanatu, yamiqa mata tad'aga kai Alamar ganewa..

kana yasake Amsa yarubuta mata bayanin nan school ne na masu fama da lalurar rasa wani b'angare nasu kamar rasa ji, gani qafafuwa hannaye D/S insunqare suxama likitoci, lauyoyi, malamai dokomaima....kinaso!? Da takaranta murmushi tayi kana Tarubuta tanaso taxama Brr....

Dayakaranta dariya yayi yace Alh y'arka Brr. Takeso taxama Also nayi mamakin yadda ta iya turanci haka Dan Wasu sekaga har Anje makarantu gaba da Scndry ba turaci" murmushi yasaki yace hakane wlh kuma insha Allahu zatazama Brr. Inba mutuwa tamin sauri ba...

Dahaka Aka ce Rayyana taje d'aukar darasi kantatatafi tajima tanayiwa Hajia da Abban muhusin kallon qurullah" amma batadubi muhusin ba dan tasan bayasonta inyaga su Hajia basa kusa harararta yakeyi..ganin kallon yayiyawa hajiya Asmau tariqe hannu rayyana tarubuta je y'arta xamudawo badajimawa ba...murmushi tayi tarungume Hajia Asma'u takuma Rungume Abban muhusin sannan tawuce...duk seda sukaji wani Irri  sabida saboo" bayan tab'acewa ganinsu Alhaji yayi godiya ga shugaban makarantar tare da roqon yakula masa ita sukayi musayar number yamasu rakkiyah sukawuce....Suna tada muto hajia tace wai Rayyanatu muh'd kodai tanaji...!?

Qwafa yayi yace tambayar dake mun yawooo kenan..!? Tace wlh natsorata kuma kaga kyakkyawan Rubutunta da harshen turanci..!? Yace nagani shiyasa nace maki y'armanayan mutanece Allah kad'ai yasan Abunda sukab'atar gurin nemanta' wlh kuwa kana suka b'arke da firar can hajia Asma'u tace dazakayi mun nasiha daka'ajiyani gidan hajia Rabi mungaisa..yace mubiya nima Inason ganin A.T.B mundad'e bamugana ba' tun Aikin damukayi dashi Ah Abuja..."

36
Tun Aikin damukayi dashi Ah Abuja....tace had'uwarku befi 3yrs ba Abban muhuseen, nifa Rabona Da hajiya Rabi tun Egypt da taje gyaran Idanu, nikuma naje Adubamun qafafuwana wata mekamawa shekaru shatakwas currrr kenan "zare ido yayi yace tabdijammmm gaskiya Shekaru sunja amma kuma banyi mamakiba tunda ga muhuseen wai 27 zaishiga.. Seda suka had'a Ido tace barikaidai Ai kwanci tashi itake qare Rai..." Yanxu ni nakai 51yrs..dariya yaqyalqyala yace Amma gaki kamar me talatin..fari tayi da Ido tace Abban muhuseen bakagajiya daxolaya" yace Allah kinfi wata y'ar 30 kyan dubiya..tace naji cigaba da yab'a mun magana..suna wannan Raha suka miqe titin Bauci Road D'od'ar suna waige Waige Amma basugane Gida ba! Alhaji yadubi hajiya yace karfa mub'ata Cikin garinnan ni narasa tai'na Zamu kamo hanya..!?

Tace kakira Number ATB mana" 

yace okh niharkinsa namanta ma da Akwai waya bari nalalubo..! Yasaka hannu gefen Aljihu yad'auko wayarsa ya dannah haske yafitooo ya bincika Sunan yadannah kira..d'iii, d'iii d'ii har takatse ba'adaukaba..yasake Dannah kiran ba'adauka ba yace hajiya qaqaxamuyi tundake bakida number hajja Rabi..!?

Tace muwuce Ai zamugana Watarana tunda y'ar mu nagarinsu" dawannan shawarar Suka juya hancin mota Titn da xaisadasu Da Amanawa.. d'orad'ar suka miqeeee hanaya..bayan mintuna talatin kiran baraya Yashigo" Abban muhuseen yad'auka sukagaisa yashaida masa ziyara sukakawo Gidan yab'ace masu..sosai yaciza leb'e kana yace kuna Ina yanxu..!? Murmushi yasaki yace gamunan Tureta munkamo hanyar Gida..." 

Qeya yasosa kamar sunaganins yace wlh har Ga Allah banji dad'iba amma zamuhad'u Soon" kagaidamin Hajiya dayarona" qyalqyalawa yayi yace Duka munatare dasu suna Amsawa" yace Ahhhhh to bani mugaisa! yabasu duka sukagaisa dashi" sannan yabawa hajia Rabi waya sukagaisa da Asama'u dagabisani suka katse line..." hajia Rabi tace Wlh banji dad'iba Samm" yace nima naso ganin Abban muhuseen Thom Amma Rigimar zuciyata ta isheni Ina ganinkiran but nakasa D'auka wlh...!? 

Hajiya cikin murya me sanyi tace nima Abun yafara kwancemun kai Allah daiyamana jagora" shiru bece komaiba Abubiyu kemasa Gezau kalaman Jabeer dana muh'd..jin bece uppanba itama tatsuke bakinta" Shiru yaratsa me wuyar fassarah da misali ga Zuciyar me kallonsu....

Bayan Sati d'aya Gefen jabeer beji motsin komai ba ga Alhaji" kana zuciyarsa nagayamasa Alhaji yamaida shi Shashasha qwallone kawai yake dahankalinshi" Afili kuma yasaki murmushi yace dani yakezancen" yamiqe zaune daga kwancen dayake yayotunanin Abunda Alhaji yafiso Aduniya" yasaki wani bahagon Murmushi ganin qwaqwalwarsa na nunamasa Abunda yagabata yanajin kamar yaune sukaje gurin Mlm...lokacin zab'en baya da Akace Alhaji baraya Cikin biyuuuu se yabada D'aya sanna zaihaye karagar mukki wanda bakowabane se uwarsa da matarsa hajia Rabi" Amma sabida son hajia Rabi yayikasadar bada u'war datakawoshi Duniya..kana wani b'angaren yaqara jaddada masacewa Lallai kashe hajia Rabi matakin Nasarane, sabida tana kan bawa y'arta shawara ta Aureshi" wanda bayan mutuwarta yayi Imani salmah zatayi biyayyah garuhin hajia Ta Aureshi ko batasoooo"

Dawannan yamiqe kasancewar Dazafi Zafi Akan Daki qarfe" yasure mota Aguje bexame Ko Inaba se Gidan haki" gurine dayatara kangararru yara matasa masujiran Ko takwana" yayi parking but befitoba sune sukayo kan motar cikin Muryoyin su na b'ata gari...yasauke Gilass kad'an yayi qasa da murya yalabarta Akin Dayakeso" wani baqin qatone yace Angama bamu Address d'ingidan kuma Kasan Akwai kud'in Daga, Rouchi, bendazo, dasauran Kayan had'i murmushi yayi iya leb'e yabasu sunan Unguwa da number Gidan yakuma Basu Yadda tsarin gidan yake tayadda zasushiga Sashen hajiya harsufito Wani beganiba...yaciro Rafar naira dubu dubu yace Atab'a kayan harka insunyin Aikin susaka farashin da sukesooo....zaibiyasu

Yajuyooo Gida Abunsa..yazauna jiran sakamako" Gidan Alhaji tukur bayan yayi sallar magrib yakira salmah yace tabbas yabata sati d'aya ta tilasta muh'd Amuncewa inbahaka ba ta d'auki Qaddarar Auren jabeer.

qasa tayi dakai sosai guntuwar qwallah tad'igo mata ganin hajiarta ma Nagoyon Bayan dady dukk dabatace komaiba! tafahimci haka Sannan cikin wata murya tace Dad! hajiya kuyi haquri nasan kunajin d'acin bijirewata" Amma bayadda na iya Domin babu Alqawarin Rabuwata da Muh'd cikin Raina Dukkkk Rintsi da juyawar zamani nayi Alqawarin kasancewa taredashi kuma Wlh koda na Auri jabeer Gangar jikina kawai zaimora Zuciyata da Ruhina sunatare da shaihiiii...

37
Gangar Jikina kawai zaimora zuciyata Da Ruhina sunatare da shaihi"......

Dukansu bawanda yasamu Arzikin motsawa bare yamaida mata martanin maganarta" Tsayin lokaci tamiqe Zuciya cunkushe da Takaicin Iyayen dabasa gane Damuwar y'ay'ansu! koda kuwa yaran masu biyayyah ne garesu hawaye nabarin qwayar Idanuwanta suna birgima Afatar fuskarta sedai batadamu dasharewa ba' kana tafe take kanta nawani fisgaggen Zafi gefe d'aya..Dakyar tasamu kaiwa D'akinta tazube Ruffff da ciki tasaki kuka me sauti, lokaci metsawoooo" sannan tadawo dashi ciki, ciki"

 Awannan yanayin hajiya Rabi tashigo tazauna daffffff daqafafuwanta tayi magana Salma batamotasa ba....hakan yasanya takai hannu tadafa jikinta" Gabanta yayi mugun fad'uwa jin Zafin jikin salma yakai 38.40c!!!batatsaya tunanin komaiba ta tallafota cikin murya me kwantar da Hankali tace Salma...!

 Kad'an ta iya d'aga makaran Idanuwanta Amma bata furta komai ba....se hajiyace tace salmah kisawa Ranki Inuwa munfi kowa son ganin farincikin ki kana inada yaqinin  babu wani mahaluqi dakeson ki tamkarmu" hannu hajiya tariqo batare da bud'e Idoba tace Hajiya kefa u'wace kinsan menene Dafinso!? But kai na nemeka bansamu ba Amma ita bannemeta ba" sabida Abunda baraya yamun nakasa mantawa" Ahamad baraya yacucemu cuter da se Alahira ubangiji yamuna hisabin gaskiya...humm Ahamad sabida baraya mahaifiyarmu ta kashe kanta"narasa k'anwata tsayin lokaci" tarasa budurcinta" tazama kurma sabida bala'in Rayuwa nazama makaho...kullum zuciyata zafi" haka dai yacigaba da zayyana masa dukk Abunda yafaru har yau damuke ciki

Ahmad Ina mata Fatan Alkhairi"
